def lse_ploss(z, p_cache? no):
   n=len(z)+1
   x=np.empty(n); x[:-1]=z; x[-1]=0
   x = x - np.max(x)
   p=np.exp(x); p/=p.sum()
   c=convolve_fft(p,p)
   # LSE with shift maxc
   maxc=c.max()
   tau=...
   if tau small, lse = maxc + logsumexp(tau*(c-maxc))/tau
   q=np.exp(tau*(c-maxc)); q/=q.sum()
   grad_p=2*np.correlate(q,p,mode='full')[n-1:n-1+n]
   grad_x=p*(grad_p - p.dot(grad_p))
   grad_z=grad_x[:-1]
   return lse, grad_z
```

```
def lp_local_search(p, time_budget=60.0, max_active_frac=0.2, verbose=False):
    p = normalize(p)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_obj=exact_obj(p); start=time.monotonic()
    box = 0.02  # absolute step bound
    active_frac = 0.05  # top 5%? maybe 0.1
    stagnation=0
    while time<deadline:
        c=conv(p); M=c.max()
        q_count = max(1, int((2*n-1)*max(1, active_frac)))
        active = np.argsort(c)[-q_count:]
        A = build active grad rows
        b = M - c[active]
        # allow small slack for non max? since active top include max
        A_ub = hstack(A, -ones)
        bounds = [(lower_i, upper_i)] + [(None,None)]
        res = linprog(...)
        if ok:
            delta=res.x[:-1]
            # line search theta
            best_theta=0; best_local=best_obj
            for theta in [1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.4, 0.25, 0.125, 0.06]:
                p_new = p + theta*delta
                p_new = np.maximum(p_new, 0); normalize
                obj=exact_obj(p_new)
                if obj<best_local*(1-1e-13): ...
            if theta>0 and obj<best_obj:
                update; box *= 1.0? maybe keep; stagnation=0
            else:
                stagnation+=1
                box *= 0.7
                if box < 1e-4: break
                active_frac = min(1.0, active_frac*1.5)
        else:
            # fallback gradient subgradient
        # periodic random kick if stagnation > 20
```

FW algorithm for dual:
```
y = uniform over 2n-1
for it:
    H = build_hankel(y)
    w,u = eigh(H, driver='evr', subset_by_index=[0,0]) # smallest eig
    z = w[0]
    g = np.zeros(2n-1)
    # gradient of z wrt y_m = sum_{i+j=m} u_i u_j = convolve(u,u)
    g = np.convolve(u,u, mode='full')  # u can signed; gradient can sign
    # conditional gradient for maximizing concave z: vertex = argmax g
    m = argmax(g)
    # step size: standard 2/(t+2) or line search
    t=1; alpha=2/(t+2)
    y_new = (1-alpha) y + alpha * e_m
    # since objective concave, line search can improve; but alpha works
    y=y_new
```

Projected simplex function top-level:
```
def project_simplex(v, target=1.0):
    # v 1D
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, len(u)+1) > (cssv - target))[0][-1]
    theta = (cssv[rho] - target) / (rho + 1.0)
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
```
(For target sum).

Dual optimize:
```
def dual_projected_gradient(n, time_budget, init_y=None):
    m=2*n-1
    y = init_y or np.ones(m)/m
    y = project_simplex(y)
    best_z=-np.inf; best_y=y.copy(); best_u=None
    step=0.1  # relative
    start=time.monotonic()
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time>budget-break
       H = build_hankel(y,n)
       w,u = smallest_eigh(H)
       z=w[0]
       if z>best_z: update
       g = np.convolve(u,u,'full')  # length m
       # project gradient to tangent? For simplex, projected gradient uses projection of y+step*g.
       # Scale g: since y sum=1, g sum=(sum u)^2. Could be ~? Use step_abs = eta / max(1, np.max(np.abs(g)))
       normg = np.max(np.abs(g))
       if normg < 1e-15: break
       eta = step / normg  # absolute move
       for factor in [1,0.5,0.25,...]:
            y_new=project_simplex(y + factor*eta * g) # but if normg scaled, factor*?
            H_new=... w_new
            if w_new > z - 1e-14: accept break
       eta_decay? 
    return best_y,best_u
```

Build H efficiently:
```
def build_hankel_vec(y, n):
    H = np.empty((n,n))
    for i in range(n):
        H[i, :] = y[i:i+n]
    return H
```
Because H[i,j]=y[i+j]. For row i, j 0..n-1 => y[i..i+n-1]. Good. O(n^2).

Implement `lp_local_search` with `current` and `best`.

Pseudo detail:
```
def lp_local_search(p_init, time_budget, start_box=0.01, verbose=False):
    p = normalize(p_init)
    best_p=p; best_obj=exact(p)
    c=...
    q_frac=0.08
    box=start_box
    stagn=0
    it=0
    while time<deadline:
       it+=1
       c=conv(p); M=c.max()
       m=2*n-1
       q=max(1, int(q_frac*m))
       active = argsort(c)[-q:]
       A=...
       b=M - c[active]
       # Maybe add small slack 1e-12
       A_ub = np.concatenate([A, -np.ones((q,1))], axis=1)
       b_ub=b
       A_eq = np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1
       bounds = []; for val in p: lower=-min(val, box); upper=box; bounds.append((lower,upper)); bounds.append((None,None))
       res=linprog(np.zeros(n)+[1], A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, 0, bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit':0.2,'presolve':True})
       if res.status==0:
           delta=res.x[:n]
           best_theta=0; best_local=best_obj
           # If t = res.x[n] > -1e-12 maybe still try smaller? Use
           for theta in [1,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.15,0.075,0.03]:
              p_new = p + theta*delta
              if np.any(p_new < -1e-12): continue
              p_new = np.maximum(p_new,0); normalize
              obj=exact(p_new)
              if obj < best_local*(1-1e-13): best_local=obj; best_theta=theta; best_new=p_new
           if best_theta>0:
              p=best_new; if obj<best_obj: update best; stagn=0
              # optional increase box if stagn==0? box=min(box*1.1, 0.05)
           else:
              stagn+=1; ...
       else:
           # fallback gradient
       if stagn>...: perturb current
```
Need if `best_local` is current obj? We want accept if better than best_obj, not just current? We can accept if better than current_p obj to maintain descent; but to find best, compare to best_obj. Use `best_obj` for global. But if current not best, line search may not improve best but improve current. We'll maintain current p maybe not best. For simplicity, in each loop p is current (which we update only if improves current). At end best may be from earlier. If stuck, set p to perturbed best. Good.

Compute current_obj each iteration? M. `current_obj=2nM`. Use it for line search: accept if new_obj < current_obj*(1-1e-13). Then also update best if new_obj<best_obj. This ensures descent on current. If no descent, adapt.

Fallback first-order from current p:
```
def softmax_dir(p, tau=1/(10*max(c,eps))):
  c=conv(p); M=...; q=np.exp(tau*(c-M)); q/=sum
  grad_p = 2*correlate(q,p,'full')[n-1:n-1+n]
  grad_x = grad_p - p.dot(grad_p)
  for step in [0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]: p_new=p*np.exp(-step*grad_x); normalize; if obj<current accept break
```
Since p positive. If p has zeros, exp(gradient) okay if p zero stays zero; maybe not escape. Can add eps: p_plus=p+1e-9/ n.

Perturbation:
```
def random_perturb(p, scale, rng):
    p_new = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0, scale, n))
    normalize
    return p_new
```

Random init function:
```
def random_init(n, seed):
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   choice=int(rng.integers(0,8))
   if choice==0: a=np.ones(n); f=rng.uniform(1,5); a += rng.uniform(0.1,0.5)*np.cos(2π f x + rng.uniform(0,2π)); ... add harmonic
   elif choice==1: a=(1+α*x)**p normalized positive
   elif choice==2: sum of 1-3 gaussians with random centers/widths
   elif choice==3: random B-spline: generate low freq Fourier coefficients decaying, a=1+sum amp cos/sin
   ...
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-6); return normalize(a)
```
We can generate 100 random inits and quick LP (e.g., 5s each) maybe too many. We'll do adaptive: sort initial by exact obj, take top 10; then random generation loop: for t in time, generate 5 inits, quick assess, if promising run short LP.

Let's implement `compute_convolution` efficiently:
```
def compute_convolution(a):
    n=len(a)
    m=2*n-1
    size=1
    while size < m: size <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)
    return conv[:m]
```
For p sum1, values small; dtype float64.

`score_from_prob(p)`:
```
n=len(p)
c=compute_convolution(p)
maxc=float(np.max(c))
return float(2.0*n*maxc)
```
If n=0 return inf.

But if cap changes, maybe score. For internal starting, use cap to mimic evaluator? If parent >1000, evaluator caps. To be consistent, cap at 1000. But scale invariance fails with cap. We should normalize after cap, i.e., arr = min(1000, max(0, arr)); then sum. This matches evaluator scale? Evaluator caps before sum. If we scale parent to avoid cap, different. Since objective scale invariant only if no cap. Parent values unknown maybe <=1000? likely. To be safe, for internal candidates we want avoid cap by scaling to max<=1000. `random_init` details. We want diverse. Use x=(0.5+np.arange(n))/n or arange(n)/(n-1). Let x from 0 to1.
```
def random_init(n, seed):
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
  choice=int(rng.integers(0, 8))
  if choice==0:
     a=np.ones(n)
     for _ in range(int(rng.integers(1,4))):
       f=float(rng.uniform(0.5,6.0)); amp=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.7)); ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       a += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x+ph)
  elif choice==1:
     p=float(rng.uniform(0.5,3.0)); c=float(rng.uniform(0.3,0.7)); w=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.4))
     a=1.0 + float(rng.uniform(0.3,1.5))*np.exp(-0.5*((x-c)/w)**2)
  elif choice==2:
     # two gaussians near edges
     c1=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.3)); c2=1.0-c1; w1=...; w2=...
     a=np.exp(-0.5*((x-c1)/w1)**2)+np.exp(-0.5*((x-c2)/w2)**2)
  elif choice==3:
     # polynomial
     a0=float(rng.uniform(0.5,2)); a1=float(rng.uniform(-1,1)); a2=float(rng.uniform(-1,1)); a3=float(rng.uniform(-1,1))
     a=a0 + a1*(2*x-1) + a2*(6*x*x-6*x+1) + a3*(10*x*x*x-15*x*x+6*x? ) # not important
  elif choice==4:
     # random smooth Fourier
     a=np.zeros(n)
     for k in range(1,6): amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.25)/k); freq=k*int(rng.integers(1,3)); ph=rng.uniform(0,2π); a+=amp*np.cos(2π freq x+ph)
     a+=1.0
  elif choice==5:
     # power ramp mixed
     alpha=rng.uniform(0,1); p1=rng.uniform(0,3); p2=rng.uniform(0,3)
     a=alpha*(x)**p1 + (1-alpha)*(1-x)**p2 + 1e-3
  elif choice==6:
     # sine/cos anti-symmetric
     f=rng.uniform(1,4); a=1+0.6*np.sin(2π f x + rng.uniform(0,2π)) + 0.3*np.cos(4π f x)
  else:
     a=np.exp(-((x-0.5)**2)/rng.uniform(0.01,0.25))
  a=np.maximum(a,1e-6); return normalize_probability(a)
```
Need values positive.

Active matrix build slice function:
```
def build_gradient_rows(p, active):
    n=len(p); q=len(active); A=np.zeros((q,n), dtype=float)
    for r,k in enumerate(active):
        k=int(k)
        i0=max(0, k-n+1); i1=min(k, n-1)
        if i1>=i0:
            j_end = k - i0
            j_start = k - i1
            if j_start == 0:
                A[r, i0:i1+1] = 2.0 * p[j_end::-1]
            else:
                A[r, i0:i1+1] = 2.0 * p[j_end:j_start-1:-1]
    return A
```
Check with k=0: i0=0,i1=0, j_end=0,j_start=0, j_start==0, p[0::-1]=[p0]. good.
k=n: i0=1,i1=n-1, j_end=n-1,j_start=1? k-i0=n-1, k-i1=1. j_start=1 => p[n-1:0:-1] gives n-1,...,1. For row k=n, valid i=1..n-1, p[n-1],...,p[1]. good.
k=2n-2: i0=n-1,i1=n-1, j_end=n-1,j_start=n-1, j_start !=0 (unless n-1=0), p[n-1:n-2:-1] -> [p[n-1]]? Stop n-2 exclusive, with step -1 includes start only. good.

`smallest_eigenpair(H)`:
```
try:
   from scipy.linalg import eigh
   w,u=eigh(H, subset_by_index=[0,0], driver='evr')
   return w[0], u[:,0]
except:
   w,u=np.linalg.eigh(H); return w[0],u[:,0]
```
`scipy.linalg.eigh` subset may require `overwrite_a=False`. Pass `check_finite=False` for speed.

         p_opt, sc2=lp_local_search(p, time_budget=min(45, remaining*0.1), seed=...)
         update
```

Implementation:
```
def dual_projected_gradient(n, time_budget, y_init=None, seed=0, verbose=False):
    m=2*n-1
    if y_init is None or not finite: y=np.ones(m)/m
    else: y=project_simplex(y)
    H=build_hankel(y,n); z,u=smallest_eigenpair(H)
    best_z=z; best_y=y.copy(); best_u=u.copy()
    step_coeff=0.05
    rng=...
    stagn=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    while time<deadline:
       it+=1
       g=np.convolve(u,u,'full')
       g = g - np.mean(g)
       normg=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       if normg < 1e-14: break
       accepted=False
       for coeff in [step_coeff, step_coeff*0.5, step_coeff*0.25, step_coeff*0.1]:
           step = coeff / normg
           y_trial = project_simplex(y + step * g)
           H_trial=build_hankel(y_trial,n)
           z_trial, u_trial=smallest_eigenpair(H_trial)
           if z_trial > z - 1e-13:
               y=y_trial; z=z_trial; u=u_trial; accepted=True; break
       if accepted:
           if z > best_z + 1e-14: best_*; stagn=0
           else: stagn+=1
       else:
           # random perturbation or reduce step
           step_coeff *= 0.6
           if step_coeff < 1e-5:
               # small random
               y = project_simplex(y + rng.normal(0, 1e-5, m))
               z, u = smallest_eigenpair(build_hankel(y,n))
           else:
               stagn += 5
       if stagn > 200:
           # restart with mix of best and random/uniform
           y=project_simplex(0.5*best_y +0.5*project_simplex(rng.random(m)))
           z,u=...
           step_coeff=0.05; stagn=0
       if verbose and it%50==0: print(...)
    return best_y,best_z,best_u
```

```
def lse_mirror_polish(p, time_budget, seed=0):
    p=normalize(p); best=p; best_obj=score; tau=1000? dynamic
    step=20.0
    start=time
    stagn=0
    while time<deadline:
       c=conv(p); M=max(c); tau=min(10000, max(10, 50/M?))
       weights=exp(tau*(c-M)); normalize
       grad_p=2*correlate(weights,p,'full')[...]
       grad_x=grad_p - p.dot(grad_p)
       # multiplicative line search
       improved=False
       for s in [step, step/2,...]:
           p_new=p*np.exp(-s*grad_x); normalize; obj=score
           if obj < best_obj-...: accept; improved=True; break
       if improved: update; stagn=0
       else:
          stagn++; if stagn>20: break or random perturb
       # adapt step: if improved many, step*=0.999? if not, step*=0.7
    return best
```

def lp_local_search(p_init, time_budget=60.0, seed=0, verbose=False):
    p = make_prob(p_init)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_obj=score_from_prob(p); current_obj=best_obj
    n=len(p); m=2*n-1
    start_box = max(1e-5, 2.0/n)
    box = start_box
    q_frac = 0.15
    base_noise=0.03
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start=time.monotonic()
    it=0; stagn=0
    while time < deadline:
       it+=1
       # If current too far from best, maybe reset
       if current_obj > best_obj * 1.02 and it>20:
           p=best_p.copy(); current_obj=best_obj
       # compute c, M
       c=compute_convolution(p); M=float(np.max(c)); current_obj=2nM
       if current_obj < best_obj: update best
       if it % 100 ==0 and verbose: print
       # choose active
       q = max(1, int(q_frac*m))
       if q > m: q=m
       active = np.argsort(c)[-q:]
       # build A
       A = build_gradient_rows(p, active)
       b = M - c[active]; b=np.maximum(b,0)
       # linear constraints
       A_ub = np.empty((q, n+1)); A_ub[:,:n]=A; A_ub[:,n]=-1
       b_ub=b
       A_eq = np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1
       c_obj=np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=1
       bounds=[]
       for val in p:
           lower = -min(val, box)
           upper = box
           bounds.append((lower, upper))
       bounds.append(( -np.inf, np.inf))
       res=linprog(...)
       if res.status==0:
           delta=res.x[:n]
           if np.max(np.abs(delta)) > 1e-15:
               best_theta=0; best_local=current_obj; best_new=None
               for theta in [1.0, 0.75, 0.5, 0.35, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05]:
                   p_new = p + theta*delta
                   if np.min(p_new) < -1e-10: continue
                   p_new = np.maximum(p_new,0); p_new=normalize(p_new)
                   obj=score_from_prob(p_new)
                   if obj < best_local*(1-1e-13):
                       best_local=obj; best_theta=theta; best_new=p_new
                       break? We could continue to find better; but break at first accepted for speed? Since θ descending, first may not best. We can try all and choose best. But all 7 evaluations, each FFT; okay. Use choose best.
               if best_new is not None:
                   p=best_new; current_obj=best_local; update best; stagn=0
                   # if small improvement many, maybe box *=1.05? not needed
               else:
                   stagn += 1
           else:
               stagn += 1
       else:
           # fallback first-order
           p_new = first_order_step(p, start_step=20.0, current_obj=current_obj)
           if p_new is not None:
               obj=score_from_prob(p_new)
               if obj < current_obj*(1-1e-13): p=p_new; current_obj=obj; update; stagn=0
               else: stagn+=1
           else:
               stagn+=1
       # adapt
       if stagn > 20:
           box *= 0.7
           q_frac = min(1.0, q_frac*1.4)
       if stagn > 60:
           # random perturbation from best
           scale = base_noise * (0.5 + 0.5*(1 - min(stagn,200)/200.0))
           p_pert = best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0, scale, n))
           p_pert = normalize(p_pert)
           obj_pert=score_from_prob(p_pert)
           if obj_pert < current_obj or obj_pert < best_obj*1.01:
               p=p_pert; current_obj=obj_pert
           else:
               # try smaller
               scale*=0.3
               p_pert=...
           box=start_box
           q_frac=0.15
           stagn=20
       if box < 1e-7:
           break
    return best_p,best_obj
```

def first_order_step(p, start_step=20.0, current_obj=None):
   if current_obj is None: current_obj=score_from_prob(p)
   c=compute_convolution(p); M=max
   tau = min(20000.0, max(50.0, 50.0/max(M,1e-12)))
   shifted=tau*(c-M); shifted=np.clip(shifted,-50,50)
   w=np.exp(shifted); w/=w.sum()
   grad_p=2*np.correlate(w,p,mode='full')[n-1:n-1+n]
   grad_x=grad_p - np.dot(p,grad_p)
   if max(abs(grad_x))<1e-14: return None
   scale= start_step / max(abs(grad_x))? To get O(start_step) multiplicative change. Actually start_step absolute for log p. Use steps = [s / max(1, np.max(np.abs(grad_x))) for s in [0.5,0.2,0.05,0.02,0.005]]? Simpler: normalize grad to max 1, steps [1,0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05].
   gnorm=np.max(np.abs(grad_x)); if gnorm<...
   for s in [1.0,0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01]:
       p_new=p*np.exp(- (s/gnorm)*grad_x)
       normalize; obj=score; if obj<current_obj*(1-1e-13): return p_new
   return None
```

```
def resample_probability(p, new_n):
    old_k=np.arange(len(p)); new_k=np.linspace(0,len(p)-1,new_n); a=np.interp(new_k,old_k,p); return make_prob(a)
```

def candidate_from_eigenvector(u):
    p1=make_prob(np.abs(u))
    p2=make_prob(np.maximum(u,0.0))
    p3=make_prob(np.maximum(-u,0.0))
    return [p1,p2,p3]
```

`project_simplex` implementation for target 1. Need handle all negative. Use standard:
```
def project_simplex(v, target_sum=1.0):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=float)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, v.size+1) > (cssv - target_sum))[0]
    if rho.size==0: return np.zeros_like(v)
    rho=int(rho[-1])
    theta=(cssv[rho]-target_sum)/(rho+1.0)
    w=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones_like(v)/v.size
    return w/s
```
Good.

Build hankel:
```
def build_hankel(y,n):
    m=len(y)
    H=np.empty((n,n), dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        H[i,:]=y[i:i+n]
    return H
```
Since y length 2n-1, i+n-1 max 2n-2 valid.

Smallest eigenpair:
```
def smallest_eigenpair(H):
    try:
       from scipy.linalg import eigh
       w,u=eigh(H, subset_by_index=[0,0], eigvals_only=False, check_finite=False, driver='evr')
       return float(w[0]), u[:,0]
    except Exception:
       w,u=np.linalg.eigh(H)
       return float(w[0]), u[:,0]
```
`subset_by_index` may require `scipy.linalg.eigh` imported; top-level import? We can import `from scipy.linalg import eigh` at top. Use it. If not, fallback.

`dual_projected_gradient` with line search. Need to compute current `z,u` initially. In loop, after accepted, skip recomputing at top? We can store. For simplification, recompute at top each iteration maybe double. Let's maintain.

```
def dual_projected_gradient(n, time_budget, y_init=None, seed=0, verbose=False):
    m=2*n-1
    rng=...
    if y_init is None: y=np.ones(m)/m
    else: y=project_simplex(y_init)
    H=build_hankel(y,n); z,u=smallest_eigenpair(H)
    best_z=z; best_y=y.copy(); best_u=u.copy()
    step_coeff=0.1
    base_step=0.1
    stagn=0
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_budget
    while time < deadline:
       g = np.convolve(u,u,'full')
       g = g - np.mean(g)
       gnorm = np.max(np.abs(g))
       if gnorm < 1e-14: break
       accepted=False
       for coeff in [step_coeff, step_coeff*0.5, step_coeff*0.25, step_coeff*0.125]:
           if time > deadline: break
           step=coeff/gnorm
           y_trial=project_simplex(y + step*g)
           z_trial,u_trial=smallest_eigenpair(build_hankel(y_trial,n))
           if z_trial > z + 1e-13:
               y=y_trial; z=z_trial; u=u_trial; accepted=True; break
       if accepted:
           if z > best_z + 1e-13: best_z=z; best_y=y.copy(); best_u=u.copy(); stagn=0
           else: stagn+=1
       else:
           step_coeff *= 0.6
           stagn += 3
           if step_coeff < 1e-5 or stagn > 200:
               # random restart
               mix=0.5
               if best_y is not None and rng.random()<0.5:
                  y = project_simplex(mix*best_y + (1-mix)*project_simplex(rng.random(m)))
               else:
                  y = project_simplex(rng.random(m))
               z,u=smallest_eigenpair(build_hankel(y,n))
               step_coeff=base_step; stagn=0
       if verbose and (it condition) print
    return best_y,best_z,best_u
```
Need `it` increment for verbose. Add.

Line search tolerance: `z_trial > z + 1e-13` may be too strict due eigen noise. Use `1e-12`. If no improve, reduce step. Near flat, may stagnate. Good.

But because objective concave, if we require improve, we may spend many small steps. Try step_coeff 0.2? We start 0.1. Good.

Time: Each trial computes full eigenpair. If 4 trials x 200 iters =800 eigs. n=330 maybe 40s? okay.

Could we use conjugate gradient to compute smallest eigen faster? Maybe not.

Top:
```
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import linprog
from scipy.linalg import eigh
```

Let's write `make_prob` carefully:
```
def make_prob(seq):
    arr=np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size==0: return np.ones(50)
    arr=arr.reshape(-1)
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    mx=float(np.max(arr))
    if mx > 1000.0:
        arr = arr * (1000.0 / mx)
    arr=np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s=float(np.sum(arr))
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.ones_like(arr) / arr.size
    return arr / s
```
If mx=0, avoid divide. Good.

`score_from_prob`: If p has zeros, okay. If n=0, inf.
```
def score_from_prob(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,float); n=len(p)
   if n==0: return np.inf
   s=p.sum(); if s<=...: return np.inf
   p=p/s
   c=compute_convolution(p); maxc=float(np.max(c)); return 2.0*n*maxc
```
It normalizes again; p already. Could avoid double but okay.

`compute_convolution`: Use size. For n=1, size=1? m=1, while size<1 false, size=1. rfft length1 ok. conv length1.

`random_init`: Need ensure positive. We'll implement choices. Use `np.pi`.

```
def random_init(n, seed):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    choice=int(rng.integers(0, 8))
    if choice==0:
       a=np.ones(n)
       nh=int(rng.integers(1,4))
       for _ in range(nh):
          f=float(rng.uniform(1.0,8.0)); amp=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.6)); ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
          a += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x + ph)
    elif choice==1:
       c=float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.8)); w=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.35)); amp=float(rng.uniform(0.5,2.0))
       a=1.0 + amp*np.exp(-0.5*((x-c)/w)**2)
    elif choice==2:
       c1=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.35)); c2=1.0-c1; w1=float(rng.uniform(0.03,0.25)); w2=float(rng.uniform(0.03,0.25))
       a=np.exp(-0.5*((x-c1)/w1)**2)+np.exp(-0.5*((x-c2)/w2)**2)
    elif choice==3:
       a0=float(rng.uniform(0.5,2.0)); a1=float(rng.uniform(-1.0,1.0)); a2=float(rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5)); a3=float(rng.uniform(-0.3,0.3))
       t=2*x-1
       a=a0 + a1*t + a2*(3*t*t-1) + a3*(5*t*t*t-3*t)
    elif choice==4:
       a=np.zeros(n)
       for k in range(1,6):
           coeff=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.3)/k)
           freq=k*int(rng.integers(1,3))
           ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
           a += coeff*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x+ph)
       a += 1.0
    elif choice==5:
       alpha=float(rng.uniform(0.0,1.0)); p1=float(rng.uniform(0.2,3.0)); p2=float(rng.uniform(0.2,3.0))
       a = alpha*np.power(np.maximum(x,1e-6),p1) + (1-alpha)*np.power(np.maximum(1-x,1e-6),p2) + 1e-3
    elif choice==6:
       f=float(rng.uniform(1.0,5.0)); amp1=float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.8)); amp2=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.4))
       a=1.0 + amp1*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)) + amp2*np.cos(4*np.pi*f*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
    else:
       c=float(rng.uniform(0.3,0.7)); w=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.4)); base=float(rng.uniform(0.1,1.0))
       a=base + 1.0*np.exp(-0.5*((x-c)/w)**2)
    a=np.maximum(a, 1e-6)
    return make_prob(a)
```

`analytic_candidates(n)`:
```
def analytic_candidates(n):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n); t=2*x-1
    cands=[]
    cands.append(np.ones(n))
    for p in [0.5,1,1.5,2,3,4]:
       cands.append(np.power(np.maximum(x,1e-6),p)+1e-6)
       cands.append(np.power(np.maximum(1-x,1e-6),p)+1e-6)
    for q in [1,2]:
       cands.append(np.power(np.maximum(x,1e-6),q)*np.power(np.maximum(1-x,1e-6),q)+1e-6)
    for a in [-0.7,-0.4,0,0.4,0.7]:
       cands.append(1.0 + a*t)
       cands.append(1.0 + a*(3*t*t-1))
       cands.append(1.0 + 0.5*a*t + 0.3*a*(3*t*t-1))
    for c,w in [(0.2,0.15),(0.8,0.15),(0.5,0.2),(0.3,0.1),(0.7,0.1)]:
       cands.append(np.exp(-0.5*((x-c)/w)**2))
    cands.append(np.exp(-0.5*((x-0.2)/0.12)**2)+np.exp(-0.5*((x-0.8)/0.12)**2))
    cands.append(np.exp(-0.5*((x-0.5)/0.2)**2)+0.2)
    # cosine harmonic
    for f in [1,2,3]:
       cands.append(1.0 + 0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x))
       cands.append(1.0 - 0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x))
    return [make_prob(a) for a in cands]
```
Many; evaluating all score O(100*FFT n) okay. Could filter.

In main, to avoid too many candidates, evaluate all, sort, keep top 12. But if analytic includes bad uniform, okay.

Line search choose best:
```
best_theta = -1.0
best_new = None
best_local = current_obj
for theta in (1.0, 0.75, 0.5, 0.35, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.025):
    p_new = p + theta * delta
    if np.min(p_new) < -1e-10: continue
    p_new = np.maximum(p_new, 0.0)
    s_new = float(np.sum(p_new))
    if s_new <= 0: continue
    p_new = p_new / s_new
    obj = score_from_prob(p_new)
    if obj < best_local * (1.0 - 1e-13):
        best_local = obj; best_new = p_new.copy()
```

Stagnation and escape:
```
if stagn > 40:
    scale = 0.05 * (0.5 + 0.5 * ((100 - min(stagn,100)) / 100.0))
    p_pert = best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, scale, n))
    p_pert = make_prob(p_pert)
    obj_pert = score_from_prob(p_pert)
    if np.isfinite(obj_pert) and (obj_pert < current_obj or obj_pert < best_obj * 1.02):
        p = p_pert; current_obj = obj_pert
    else:
        scale *= 0.25
        p_pert = best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, scale, n)); p_pert=make_prob...
        p = p_pert; current_obj = score_from_prob(p)
    box = max(1e-6, start_box)
    q_frac = 0.15
    stagn = 10
```
Could keep escaping even if perturbed obj worse than best; that's intended. But if too worse, set p to perturbed and continue; best remains. Good.

    # ensure length m

`smallest_eigh` if `eigh` subset not available? We imported `eigh`; call with `subset_by_index`. In older scipy, `eigh` may not support `driver='evr'` with subset? It should. Wrap exceptions. In fallback full `np.linalg.eigh`.

```
def smallest_eigenpair(H):
    try:
        w,u = eigh(H, subset_by_index=[0,0], check_finite=False)
        return float(w[0]), u[:,0]
    except Exception:
        try:
            w,u = eigh(H, check_finite=False)
            order=np.argsort(w)
            return float(w[order[0]]), u[:,order[0]]
        except Exception:
            w,u = np.linalg.eigh(H)
            return float(w[0]), u[:,0]
```

```python
"""
Global + local search for nonnegative step-function heights.

The objective is scale-invariant, so the search normalizes sequences to
sum 1 and minimizes 2*n*max(conv(a,a)).  Main ingredients:
  1. analytic and smooth random initializations,
  2. an active-set linearized LP search (linearize the convolution peak
     and take a constrained step),
  3. first-order softmax/practical subgradient polish,
  4. a convex dual projected-gradient heuristic on Hankel matrices to
     produce additional global candidate eigenvectors.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import linprog
from scipy.linalg import eigh


def make_prob(seq):
    """Convert an array-like to a nonnegative normalized probability vector."""
    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(50, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = arr.reshape(-1)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(arr))
    if mx > 1000.0:
        arr = arr * (1000.0 / mx)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.ones_like(arr, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def compute_convolution(a):
    """Full linear convolution a*a via FFT."""
    n = int(len(a))
    m = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1
    while size < m:
        size <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)
    return out[:m]


def score_from_prob(p):
    """Exact objective for normalized nonnegative p: 2*n*max(conv(p,p))."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(len(p))
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    p = p / s
    c = compute_convolution(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(maxc):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * maxc)


def resample_probability(p, new_n):
    """Interpolate a probability sequence to a new length."""
    old_n = int(len(p))
    new_n = int(max(1, new_n))
    if old_n == new_n:
        return p.copy()
    old_t = np.arange(old_n, dtype=np.float64)
    new_t = np.linspace(0.0, old_n - 1, new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    q = np.interp(new_t, old_t, p)
    return make_prob(q)


def get_parent_prob():
    """Fetch the global parent construction if available."""
    try:
        arr = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if arr is None:
            return None
        p = make_prob(arr)
        if p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            return p
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def random_init(n, seed):
    """Diverse smooth nonnegative initializations."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n = int(max(2, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    choice = int(rng.integers(0, 8))

    if choice == 0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        nh = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
        for _ in range(nh):
            f = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 8.0))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.6))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            a += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph)
    elif choice == 1:
        c = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        w = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0))
        a = 1.0 + amp * np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - c) / w) ** 2)
    elif choice == 2:
        c1 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
        c2 = 1.0 - c1
        w1 = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.25))
        w2 = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.25))
        a = np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - c1) / w1) ** 2) + np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - c2) / w2) ** 2)
    elif choice == 3:
        a0 = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0))
        a1 = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0))
        a2 = float(rng.uniform(-0.5, 0.5))
        a3 = float(rng.uniform(-0.3, 0.3))
        a = a0 + a1 * t + a2 * (3.0 * t * t - 1.0) + a3 * (5.0 * t * t * t - 3.0 * t)
    elif choice == 4:
        a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in range(1, 6):
            coeff = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.3)) / float(k)
            freq = k * int(rng.integers(1, 3))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            a += coeff * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + ph)
        a += 1.0
    elif choice == 5:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
        p1 = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 3.0))
        p2 = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 3.0))
        xr = np.maximum(x, 1e-6)
        xl = np.maximum(1.0 - x, 1e-6)
        a = alpha * np.power(xr, p1) + (1.0 - alpha) * np.power(xl, p2) + 1e-3
    elif choice == 6:
        f = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 5.0))
        amp1 = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        amp2 = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.4))
        ph1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        a = 1.0 + amp1 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph1) + amp2 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph2)
    else:
        c = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 0.7))
        w = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.4))
        base = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 1.0))
        a = base + np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - c) / w) ** 2)

    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-6)
    return make_prob(a)


def analytic_candidates(n):
    """A small family of smooth deterministic candidates."""
    n = int(max(2, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    out = []
    out.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for p in [0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0]:
        out.append(np.maximum(np.power(np.maximum(x, 1e-6), p), 1e-6))
        out.append(np.maximum(np.power(np.maximum(1.0 - x, 1e-6), p), 1e-6))

    for q in [1, 2]:
        out.append(
            np.maximum(x, 1e-6) ** q * np.maximum(1.0 - x, 1e-6) ** q + 1e-6
        )

    for a in [-0.7, -0.4, 0.0, 0.4, 0.7]:
        out.append(1.0 + a * t)
        out.append(1.0 + a * (3.0 * t * t - 1.0))
        out.append(1.0 + 0.5 * a * t + 0.3 * a * (3.0 * t * t - 1.0))

    gauss_specs = [
        (0.2, 0.15), (0.8, 0.15), (0.5, 0.2), (0.3, 0.10), (0.7, 0.10),
        (0.1, 0.08), (0.9, 0.08), (0.5, 0.05), (0.5, 0.35)
    ]
    for c, w in gauss_specs:
        out.append(np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - c) / w) ** 2) + 1e-6)

    out.append(
        np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - 0.2) / 0.12) ** 2) +
        np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - 0.8) / 0.12) ** 2) + 1e-6
    )
    out.append(np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - 0.5) / 0.2) ** 2) + 0.2)

    for f in [1, 2, 3]:
        out.append(1.0 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x))
        out.append(1.0 - 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x))
        out.append(1.0 + 0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x))

    return [make_prob(a) for a in out]


def build_gradient_rows(p, active):
    """
    For active convolution lags k, build rows G[k,i] = 2*p[k-i] if valid.
    """
    n = int(len(p))
    q = int(len(active))
    A = np.zeros((q, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for r, k in enumerate(active):
        k = int(k)
        i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
        i1 = min(k, n - 1)
        if i1 >= i0:
            j_end = k - i0
            j_start = k - i1
            if j_start == 0:
                A[r, i0:i1 + 1] = 2.0 * p[j_end::-1]
            else:
                A[r, i0:i1 + 1] = 2.0 * p[j_end:j_start - 1:-1]
    return A


def first_order_step(p, start_step=20.0, current_obj=None):
    """
    One practical softmax-subgradient step with exact line search.
    Returns a better p, or None.
    """
    p = make_prob(p)
    n = int(len(p))
    if current_obj is None:
        current_obj = score_from_prob(p)
    c = compute_convolution(p)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return None

    tau = min(20000.0, max(50.0, 50.0 / max(M, 1e-12)))
    shifted = tau * (c - M)
    shifted = np.clip(shifted, -50.0, 50.0)
    w = np.exp(shifted)
    ws = float(np.sum(w))
    if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        return None
    w = w / ws

    grad_p = 2.0 * np.correlate(w, p, mode="full")[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    grad_x = grad_p - float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
    gnorm = float(np.max(np.abs(grad_x)))
    if gnorm < 1e-14:
        return None

    p_safe = p + 1e-12
    ps = float(np.sum(p_safe))
    if ps <= 0:
        return None
    p_safe = p_safe / ps

    for s in [1.0, 0.5, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005]:
        p_new = p_safe * np.exp(-(s / gnorm) * grad_x)
        p_new = make_prob(p_new)
        obj = score_from_prob(p_new)
        if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < current_obj * (1.0 - 1e-13):
            return p_new
    return None


def lp_local_search(p_init, time_budget=60.0, seed=0, verbose=False):
    """
    Active-set linearized LP local search with exact line search and escape
    perturbations.  Returns (best_p, best_score).
    """
    p = make_prob(p_init)
    n = int(len(p))
    if n < 2:
        p = np.ones(10, dtype=np.float64) / 10.0
        n = 10

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_obj = score_from_prob(p)
    current_obj = best_obj
    start_box = max(1e-6, 2.0 / float(n))
    box = start_box
    q_frac = 0.15
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + max(1.0, float(time_budget))
    it = 0
    stagn = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        c = compute_convolution(p)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break
        current_obj = float(2.0 * n * M)

        if current_obj < best_obj - 1e-14:
            best_obj = current_obj
            best_p = p.copy()

        if verbose and (it % 50 == 1):
            print(f"    LP it={it} obj={current_obj:.8f} best={best_obj:.8f} box={box:.3g} q_frac={q_frac:.2f}")

        m = 2 * n - 1
        q = max(1, int(q_frac * m))
        if q > m:
            q = m
        active = np.argsort(c)[-q:]

        A = build_gradient_rows(p, active)
        b = np.maximum(M - c[active], 0.0)

        A_ub = np.empty((q, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A_ub[:, :n] = A
        A_ub[:, n] = -1.0
        b_ub = b

        A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
        b_eq = np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)

        c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        c_obj[-1] = 1.0

        bounds = []
        for val in p:
            val = float(val)
            lower = -min(val, box)
            upper = box
            bounds.append((lower, upper))
        bounds.append((-np.inf, np.inf))

        delta = None
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c, c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub,
                A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds,
                method="highs", options={"presolve": True}
            )
            if res.status == 0 and res.x is not None:
                delta = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(delta)):
                    delta = None
        except Exception:
            delta = None

        accepted = False
        if delta is not None and float(np.max(np.abs(delta))) > 1e-15:
            best_theta = -1.0
            best_new = None
            best_local = current_obj
            for theta in [1.0, 0.75, 0.55, 0.35, 0.22, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03]:
                p_new = p + theta * delta
                if float(np.min(p_new)) < -1e-10:
                    continue
                p_new = np.maximum(p_new, 0.0)
                s_new = float(np.sum(p_new))
                if s_new <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                p_new = p_new / s_new
                obj_new = score_from_prob(p_new)
                if np.isfinite(obj_new) and obj_new < best_local * (1.0 - 1e-13):
                    best_local = obj_new
                    best_new = p_new
                    best_theta = theta

            if best_new is not None:
                p = best_new.copy()
                current_obj = best_local
                if current_obj < best_obj - 1e-14:
                    best_obj = current_obj
                    best_p = p.copy()
                stagn = 0
                accepted = True
        else:
            # Fallback first-order step.
            p_new = first_order_step(p, start_step=20.0, current_obj=current_obj)
            if p_new is not None:
                obj_new = score_from_prob(p_new)
                if np.isfinite(obj_new) and obj_new < current_obj * (1.0 - 1e-13):
                    p = p_new.copy()
                    current_obj = obj_new
                    if current_obj < best_obj - 1e-14:
                        best_obj = current_obj
                        best_p = p.copy()
                    stagn = 0
                    accepted = True

        if not accepted:
            stagn += 1

        if stagn > 18:
            box = max(1e-7, box * 0.72)
            q_frac = min(1.0, q_frac * 1.35)

        if stagn > 60:
            # Escape from local basin while preserving the best incumbent.
            scale = 0.06 * (0.35 + 0.65 * (100.0 - min(float(stagn), 100.0)) / 100.0)
            p_pert = best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, scale, n))
            p_pert = make_prob(p_pert)
            obj_pert = score_from_prob(p_pert)
            if not (np.isfinite(obj_pert) and (obj_pert < current_obj or obj_pert < best_obj * 1.02)):
                scale *= 0.3
                p_pert = best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, scale, n))
                p_pert = make_prob(p_pert)
                obj_pert = score_from_prob(p_pert)

            p = p_pert
            current_obj = obj_pert if np.isfinite(obj_pert) else score_from_prob(p)
            box = max(1e-7, start_box)
            q_frac = 0.15
            stagn = 12

        if box < 1e-8:
            break

    return best_p, best_obj


def project_simplex(v, target_sum=1.0):
    """Euclidean projection onto the probability simplex."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    idxs = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, n + 1) > (cssv - target_sum))[0]
    if idxs.size == 0:
        w = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        rho = int(idxs[-1])
        theta = (cssv[rho] - target_sum) / float(rho + 1)
        w = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return w / s


def build_hankel(y, n):
    """Build the n x n Hankel matrix H[i,j]=y[i+j]."""
    n = int(n)
    H = np.empty((n, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(n):
        H[i, :] = y[i:i + n]
    return H


def smallest_eigenpair(H):
    """Return smallest eigenvalue and eigenvector of a symmetric matrix."""
    try:
        w, u = eigh(H, subset_by_index=[0, 0], check_finite=False)
        return float(w[0]), np.asarray(u[:, 0], dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        try:
            w, u = eigh(H, check_finite=False)
            order = np.argsort(w)
            return float(w[order[0]]), np.asarray(u[:, order[0]], dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            w, u = np.linalg.eigh(H)
            return float(w[0]), np.asarray(u[:, 0], dtype=np.float64)


def dual_projected_gradient(n, time_budget=60.0, y_init=None, seed=0, verbose=False):
    """
    Heuristic convex dual search:
      maximize z = lambda_min(Hankel(y)) s.t. y in simplex.
    Returns best_y, best_z, best_u.
    """
    n = int(max(2, n))
    m = 2 * n - 1
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    if y_init is None:
        y = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    else:
        y = project_simplex(y_init, target_sum=1.0)
        y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
        s = float(np.sum(y))
        if s <= 0:
            y = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
        else:
            y = y / s

    H = build_hankel(y, n)
    z, u = smallest_eigenpair(H)
    best_z = z
    best_y = y.copy()
    best_u = u.copy()

    base_step = 0.12
    step_coeff = base_step
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + max(1.0, float(time_budget))
    it = 0
    stagn = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        g = np.convolve(u, u, mode="full")
        if g.size < m:
            tmp = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
            tmp[:g.size] = g
            g = tmp
        elif g.size > m:
            g = g[:m]
        g = g - float(np.mean(g))
        gnorm = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if gnorm < 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(gnorm):
            break

        accepted = False
        for coeff in [step_coeff, step_coeff * 0.5, step_coeff * 0.25, step_coeff * 0.125]:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            step = coeff / gnorm
            y_trial = project_simplex(y + step * g, target_sum=1.0)
            z_trial, u_trial = smallest_eigenpair(build_hankel(y_trial, n))
            if np.isfinite(z_trial) and z_trial > z + 1e-12:
                y = y_trial
                z = z_trial
                u = u_trial
                accepted = True
                break

        if accepted:
            if z > best_z + 1e-12:
                best_z = z
                best_y = y.copy()
                best_u = u.copy()
                stagn = 0
            else:
                stagn += 1
        else:
            step_coeff = max(1e-5, step_coeff * 0.6)
            stagn += 2

        if verbose and (it % 20 == 1):
            print(f"    dual it={it} z={z:.8f} best_z={best_z:.8f} score_lb={2.0*n*best_z:.6f}")

        if step_coeff <= 1e-5 or stagn > 220:
            if best_y is not None and rng.random() < 0.45:
                y = project_simplex(0.5 * best_y + 0.5 * project_simplex(rng.random(m)), target_sum=1.0)
            else:
                y = project_simplex(rng.random(m), target_sum=1.0)
            z, u = smallest_eigenpair(build_hankel(y, n))
            if np.isfinite(z) and z > best_z:
                best_z = z
                best_y = y.copy()
                best_u = u.copy()
            step_coeff = base_step
            stagn = 0

    return best_y, best_z, best_u


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500.0):
    """
    Main search routine.  Returns a Python list of nonnegative heights.
    """
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + max(10.0, float(budget_s)) - 25.0
    rng_global = np.random.default_rng(seed + 1)

    best_p = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    parent = get_parent_prob()
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        base_n = int(parent.size)
    else:
        base_n = 330
    base_n = int(np.clip(base_n, 120, 800))
    print(f"Base length: {base_n}")

    # ---- Build initial candidate pool. ----
    cand = []
    idx = 0

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        cand.append((idx, "parent", parent))
        idx += 1
        cand.append((idx, "parent_rev", parent[::-1].copy()))
        idx += 1

    n_list = []
    for delta in [0, -50, 50, -100, 100, 150, -150, 200]:
        n = base_n + delta
        if 120 <= n <= 800 and n not in n_list:
            n_list.append(n)
    if base_n not in n_list:
        n_list.insert(0, base_n)

    analytic_ns = [base_n]
    if len(n_list) > 2:
        analytic_ns.append(n_list[2])

    for n in n_list:
        if parent is not None and parent.size != n:
            pr = resample_probability(parent, n)
            cand.append((idx, f"parent_{n}", pr))
            idx += 1
        for k in range(3):
            cand.append((idx, f"rand_{n}_{k}", random_init(n, seed + 1000 * n + 7 * k)))
            idx += 1

    for n in analytic_ns:
        for p in analytic_candidates(n):
            cand.append((idx, f"an_{n}_{idx}", p))
            idx += 1

    scored = []
    for _, name, p in cand:
        sc = score_from_prob(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, name, p))
    scored.sort()

    if scored:
        for sc, name, p in scored[:3]:
            print(f"Initial candidate {name}: {sc:.6f}")
        best_score = scored[0][0]
        best_p = scored[0][2].copy()
    else:
        best_p = np.ones(base_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(base_n)
        best_score = score_from_prob(best_p)
        print(f"Fallback uniform score: {best_score:.6f}")

    def_finish = 0  # unused, avoids accidental closure


    # ---- Focused local search on best initial candidates. ----
    processed = set()
    for j, (sc, name, p) in enumerate(scored):
        if len(processed) >= 10:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 350:
            break
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 30:
            break
        if name in processed:
            continue
        processed.add(name)

        if "parent" in name:
            b = min(260.0, rem * 0.32)
        elif sc <= best_score * 1.02:
            b = min(170.0, rem * 0.26)
        else:
            b = min(70.0, rem * 0.14)
        if b < 5:
            break

        print(f"\nLP {name} (score {sc:.6f}, budget {b:.0f}s)")
        p_opt, sc_opt = lp_local_search(p, time_budget=b, seed=seed + 100000 + 7919 * j, verbose=("parent" in name))
        if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc_opt)
            best_p = np.asarray(p_opt, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            print(f"  ** update: {name} -> {best_score:.8f}")

    # ---- Dual Hankel heuristic to find new global candidates. ----
    if best_p is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 450:
        n_dual = int(len(best_p))
        if n_dual > 600:
            p_small = resample_probability(best_p, 500)
            n_dual = 500
            y_init = compute_convolution(p_small)
        else:
            y_init = compute_convolution(best_p)

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        dual_time = min(260.0, rem * 0.32)
        print(f"\nDual projected-gradient search, n={n_dual}, budget={dual_time:.0f}s")
        try:
            y_dual, z_dual, u_dual = dual_projected_gradient(
                n_dual, time_budget=dual_time, y_init=y_init,
                seed=seed + 987654321
            )
            if np.isfinite(z_dual):
                print(f"  dual lower-bound objective: {2.0 * n_dual * z_dual:.8f}")

            if u_dual is not None:
                cand_dual = []
                cand_dual.append("abs(u)", np.abs(u_dual))
                cand_dual.append("pos(u)", np.maximum(u_dual, 0.0))
                cand_dual.append("neg(u)", np.maximum(-u_dual, 0.0))
                if best_p.size == n_dual:
                    for mix in [0.25, 0.5, 0.75]:
                        cand_dual.append(f"mix_{mix}", mix * best_p + (1.0 - mix) * np.abs(u_dual))

                for name, p in cand_dual:
                    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 220:
                        break
                    pp = make_prob(p)
                    sc = score_from_prob(pp)
                    if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc > best_score * 1.08:
                        continue
                    b = min(120.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.18)
                    if b < 5:
                        break
                    print(f"\nPolish dual candidate {name}: raw {sc:.6f}, budget {b:.0f}s")
                    p_opt, sc_opt = lp_local_search(pp, time_budget=b, seed=seed + 555000, verbose=False)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_score:
                        best_score = float(sc_opt)
                        best_p = np.asarray(p_opt, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                        print(f"  ** update: dual {name} -> {best_score:.8f}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"  dual failed: {e}")

    # ---- Random-restart local search. ----
    ns = [base_n]
    if base_n - 50 >= 100:
        ns.append(base_n - 50)
    if base_n + 50 <= 800:
        ns.append(base_n + 50)
    counter = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 320:
        counter += 1
        n = ns[counter % len(ns)]
        for k in range(2):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 320:
                break
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 25:
                break
            p = random_init(n, seed + 700000 * counter + 101 * k + int(rng_global.integers(0, 1000)))
            sc = score_from_prob(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and (sc < best_score * 1.12 or counter < 4):
                b = min(45.0, rem * 0.16)
                if b < 5:
                    break
                p_opt, sc_opt = lp_local_search(p, time_budget=b, seed=seed + 800000 * counter + k, verbose=False)
                if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_score:
                    best_score = float(sc_opt)
                    best_p = np.asarray(p_opt, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                    print(f"\nRandom restart {counter}.{k} n={n}: {best_score:.8f}")

    # ---- Final polishing. ----
    if best_p is not None:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 80:
            b = min(320.0, rem * 0.62)
            print(f"\nFinal LP polish, budget={b:.0f}s, current={best_score:.8f}")
            p_opt, sc_opt = lp_local_search(best_p, time_budget=b, seed=seed + 424242, verbose=True)
            if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc_opt)
                best_p = np.asarray(p_opt, dtype=np.float64).copy()

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 25:
            # A few first-order steps for cheap final improvements.
            for _ in range(8):
                p_new = first_order_step(best_p, start_step=10.0, current_obj=best_score)
                if p_new is None:
                    break
                sc_new = score_from_prob(p_new)
                if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score - 1e-13:
                    best_p = p_new
                    best_score = float(sc_new)
                else:
                    break

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 50:
            b = min(120.0, rem * 0.55)
            p_opt, sc_opt = lp_local_search(best_p, time_budget=b, seed=seed + 1234567, verbose=False)
            if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc_opt)
                best_p = np.asarray(p_opt, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = np.ones(base_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(base_n)
        best_score = score_from_prob(best_p)

    best_p = make_prob(best_p)
    result = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_p]

    # Final validation against the provided evaluator if available.
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(result)
        if np.isfinite(val):
            if val < best_score:
                best_score = float(val)
            print(f"Internal score: {score_from_prob(make_prob(result)):.8f}, evaluator: {val:.8f}")
        else:
            print("Evaluator returned non-finite; using uniform fallback.")
            best_p = np.ones(200, dtype=np.float64) / 200.0
            result = [float(x) for x in best_p]
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Evaluator validation skipped: {e}")

    print(f"FINAL: length={len(result)}, internal_best={best_score:.8f}, used_time={time.monotonic()-start_time:.1f}s")
    return result
```
